# Structural signals and remaining review queue

Every source definition was parsed and screened. A signal is a reason to inspect, not a value rating; an empty signal set is not a semantic review. Runtime duration, production call coverage, mutation coverage, inter-file semantic equivalence and complete boundary adequacy were not established for every definition.

| Signal | Definitions | Interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| stubbed-boundary | 1287 | A stub/mock/spy call appears. Faking an external boundary is expected; replacing the behavior under test is not. |
| environment-reference | 1258 | Configuration or environment access appears. Host selection is necessary input, not evidence of a bad oracle. |
| static-token-duplicate | 375 | The body token hash matches another definition. Inputs in setup, class, subject and generated parameters may differ. |
| coverage-named | 349 | The filename mentions coverage/threshold; no quality conclusion follows from naming alone. |
| redefined-subject-or-double | 267 | Method or singleton behavior is defined in the body; inspect whether it replaces the subject or a collaborator. |
| metadata-oracle | 179 | Association/validator/callback/module reflection appears; it may enforce an explicit security contract. |
| source-text-oracle | 140 | A source-reading or source-named expression appears. This heuristic can match ordinary data; required security guards remain. |
| conditional-reachability | 95 | A capability/optional existence condition can affect whether the claimed subject is reached. |
| reflective-dispatch | 74 | send/__send__ with a literal method. This does not establish private visibility or low value. |
| reflection-state | 55 | Direct instance/class variable access; distinguish harmless fixture setup from internal-state oracles. |
| broad-rescue | 40 | StandardError or Exception is rescued. Concurrency tests can validly carry errors to checked queues. |
| self-type-oracle | 4 | An assertion checks that self is a Minitest test object; inspect the remaining oracle before judging. |
| self-thrown-exception | 2 | An assert_raises block directly raises; real domain error construction can still be meaningful. |
| literal-pass | 1 | A literal assert true appears in a reachable source branch; inspect its condition. |
